need a list of products we can buy without worrying about telemetry or advertising.","body":"It is so difficult to find modern electronics without telemetry and ads. Smartphones, laptops, TVs, home routers, security cameras, cars, game consoles, e-readers, projectors, 3D printers, fitness trackers, speakers, and even some printers. Why can’t customers be free from this data collection and advertising? It’s not that we don’t care about it. There simply aren’t many alternatives, and the few that do exist are either more expensive or offer fewer features. Seriously, we need a list of products we can buy and use directly without worrying about telemetry or advertising. \n\nThe following list is updated at https:\u002F\u002Flemmy.ml\u002Fpost\u002F52706206. Please note that this list may contain errors.\n- PC: Anything Linux\n    - [Intel Backdoor](https:\u002F\u002Fwww.youtube.com\u002Fwatch?v=ZpXkJqTAY5Y)\n    - [AMD Backdoor](https:\u002F\u002Fen.wikipedia.org\u002Fwiki\u002FAMD_Platform_Security_Processor)\n- Laptop: [Framework Laptop](https:\u002F\u002Fframe.work\u002F), [System76](https:\u002F\u002Fsystem76.com\u002F), [MNT](https:\u002F\u002Fmntre.com\u002F), [Purism](https:\u002F\u002Fpuri.sm\u002F) Laptops, [Starlabs](https:\u002F\u002Fstarlabs.systems\u002Fcollections\u002Flaptops) Laptops, [TUXEDO Computers](https:\u002F\u002Fwww.tuxedocomputers.com\u002F), [NovaCustom](https:\u002F\u002Fnovacustom.com\u002F), [Slimbook](https:\u002F\u002Fslimbook.com\u002F), [Pinebook](https:\u002F\u002Fpine64.org\u002Fdevices\u002F) by [PINE64](https:\u002F\u002Fpine64.org\u002F)\n- Smartphone: [Jolla Phone](https:\u002F\u002Fcommerce.jolla.com\u002Fproducts\u002Fjolla-phone-preorder), [Purism](https:\u002F\u002Fpuri.sm\u002F), [NovaCustom Smartphones](https:\u002F\u002Fnovacustom.com\u002Fcat\u002Fsmartphones\u002F), [Fairphone with \u002Fe\u002FOS](https:\u002F\u002Fwww.fairphone.com\u002Fthe-fairphone-gen-6-e-operating-system), [PinePhone](https:\u002F\u002Fpine64.org\u002Fdevices\u002F) by [PINE64](https:\u002F\u002Fpine64.org\u002F)\n    -
